- **writeup-ingest**: Reusable bug-bounty writeup / CVE ingestion pipeline for the BB-OS skill library. Converts 2024-2026 disclosed reports, CVEs, and vendor advisories into additive, cited, deduped skill sections. Use at the start of a fresh campaign when the threat landscape for a class may have changed, after a big ecosystem event (Keycloak/Spring/DOMPurify advisory wave), or whenever a fellow hunter posts a writeup for a class you work. Complements the one-shot enrichment sprints by making ingestion repeatable. ([murrtada/bug-bounty-agent-skills](https://github.com/murrtada/bug-bounty-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/writeup-ingest) · ★7 · 4 copies)

- **writeup-template**: Use after completing any substantive security workflow with this project, including CTF, bug bounty, CVE validation, guided MCP assessment, DFIR or pentest/recon. Not for work on this repository itself (code review, tool inventories, troubleshooting the toolkit). Generates a writeup at writeups/&lt;category&gt;/&lt;descriptive-case-name&gt;.md following the project's mandatory structure. Triggers on "write writeup", "document this challenge", "write up the solve", "document this finding", or right after a flag/finding/result is confirmed. ([26zl/cybersec-toolkit](https://github.com/26zl/cybersec-toolkit/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/writeup-template) · ★100)

- **writewell-voice-onboard**: Create a personal voice profile for the writewell-with-voice skill by analyzing writing samples. Provide individual post URLs or a blog homepage URL with a post count. Use when setting up or refreshing a personal voice, or when writewell-with-voice finds no profile. ([p3ob7o/skillpack](https://github.com/p3ob7o/skillpack/tree/HEAD/plugins/writewell-with-voice/skills/writewell-voice-onboard) · ★3)
- **writewell-with-voice**: Full writing pipeline: strip AI patterns, enforce language discipline, remove clutter, verify quality, and optionally apply a personal voice. ([p3ob7o/skillpack](https://github.com/p3ob7o/skillpack/tree/HEAD/plugins/writewell-with-voice/skills/writewell-with-voice) · ★3)

- **writin-skills**: Use when creating a new skill, editing a SKILL.md, fixing the description of a skill that doesn't trigger or triggers at the wrong time, reviewing someone else's skill, or checking that a skill works before trusting it — including when the request doesn't say "skill" ("esse prompt não está pegando", "transforma isso num processo reutilizável"). Do NOT use for writing a one-off prompt, a CLAUDE.md or a memory that won't become a skill, nor for creating an agent or a plugin. ([cassianodiniz/cass](https://github.com/cassianodiniz/cass/tree/HEAD/skills/writin-skills) · ★4)
- **writing**: 将共享历史中的已验证事实和计算结果整理成符合受众、格式与长度约束的成稿。 ([bojieli/ai-agent-book](https://github.com/bojieli/ai-agent-book/tree/HEAD/chapter10/multi-role-transfer/skills/writing) · ★59k · 4 copies)
- **writing** ([wu-yc/LabClaw](https://github.com/wu-yc/LabClaw/tree/HEAD/skills/general/writing) · ★1.1k · 6 copies)
- **writing**: Write prose people will actually read. Use for any prose you produce - docs, READMEs, release notes, blog posts, PR descriptions, issue text, commit bodies, design docs, emails, chat answers. Applies Orwell's six rules and strips the LLM tells - passive voice, dead metaphors, and -ly padding. ([agentic-community/mcp-gateway-registry](https://github.com/agentic-community/mcp-gateway-registry/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/writing) · ★1.1k · 2 copies)

- **writing**: 文章を書く・推敲するときに読む。ドキュメント、コメント、コミットメッセージ、PR 本文、記事のような説明文と、小説のような読み物が対象である。「AI っぽい」「読みにくい」文章を直すときにも使う。 ([aiskillstore/marketplace](https://github.com/aiskillstore/marketplace/tree/HEAD/skills/masseater/writing) · ★487 · 2 copies)
- **writing**: Writing: voice creation and validation, prose editing, anti-AI cleanup, professional communication, translation. ([notque/vexjoy-agent](https://github.com/notque/vexjoy-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/content/writing) · ★451 · 2 copies)
- **writing**: Draft, revise, audit, or transform human-facing prose: articles, blogs, documentation, criticism, essays, email, marketing and SEO copy, summaries, scripts, application materials, and UI text. Excludes code comments, commit messages, and private notes. ([Anbeeld/WRITING.md](https://github.com/Anbeeld/WRITING.md/tree/HEAD/skills/writing) · ★391 · 2 copies)
- **writing**: Turn a short instruction, supplied material or selected topic into a useful draft, rewrite or summary without unnecessary setup questions. ([socai-io/socai](https://github.com/socai-io/socai/tree/HEAD/core/src/agent/skills/writing) · ★258)
- **writing**: 文案撰寫全套工作流：分析受眾語調、套用說服公式（AIDA/PAS/FAB）、產出部落格/Email/Landing Page/社群文案，並提供 SEO 優化建議。適合在撰寫行銷文案、內容行銷、或需要匹配特定品牌語調時使用。 ([zeuikli/claude-code-workspace](https://github.com/zeuikli/claude-code-workspace/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/writing) · ★157)
- **writing**: 通用写作技能：以"内容→组件→组合"的方式产出技术文档、产品文档、汇报材料。 覆盖 spec / plan / PRD / 用户手册 / 项目汇报 / 方案路演 / 调研报告 等所有结构化文档场景。 核心理念：组件驱动，不预设模板。先看内容形态，再挑能装这种形态的组件， 组合自然成文——不强制章节结构、不预设"必备模块"。 组件库（HTML / Markdown / 图示）见 references/components.md， 常见组合范式（spec / plan / PRD 等）见 references/templates/——但模板是参考不是规定。 触发词：写文档、写方案、写计划、写 PRD、写汇报、项目总结、写指南、帮我写、文档、方案设计。 即使用户没有明确说"写"，只要意图是"产出一份结构化的文档/报告/材料"，都应触发此技能。 本 skill 只负责"写"，不负责"调研"（调研由 deep-research 承担）和"调度"（流程由 task-start 承担）。 ([312362115/claude](https://github.com/312362115/claude/tree/HEAD/skills/writing) · ★107)
- **Writing**: Iterative critique and improvement of long-form content (guidebooks, playbooks, essays).
